## Corpus Check
- 2323 files · ~2,549,811 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 343 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .xsd 117, .ttf 108, .csv 53)

## Summary
- 31278 nodes · 44898 edges · 1923 communities (1636 shown, 287 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 1621 edges (avg confidence: 0.89)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `c5427956`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
